relevant to the Group’s business and its key stakeholders.                      對被識別為對本集團業務及其主要持份者屬
                                                                                重要及相關之層面 。

A. Environmental Protection                                                     A. 環境保護
     The Group undertakes environmental protection as part of its corporate         本集團將環境保護視為其企業責任之一
     responsibilities, and it is fully aware of the importance of sustainable       部分 ，並深悉可持續環境發展對促進其
     environmental development in promoting sustainability in its operations.       營運可持續性之重要性 。本集團致力於
     The Group is committed to implementing different environmental                 實施不同環保措施 ，以減輕對環境之影

Board Diversity                                                                    董事會成員多元化
The Board has established a policy setting out the approach to achieve             董事會已制定一項政策 ，載列董事會實現多
diversity on the Board (the “Board Diversity Policy”) with the aims of enhancing   元 化 之 方 法（「 董 事 會 成 員 多 元 化 政 策 」）， 旨
Board effectiveness and corporate governance as well as achieving better           在提高董事會效率 、加強企業管治及提升本
performance of the Company. In designing the board composition, board              公司表現 。設定董事會之成員組合時會從多
diversity can be achieved through consideration of a number of factors,            項因素考慮董事會成員多元化 ，包括但不限
including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and education background,       於性別、年齡、文化和教育背景、專業技
professional skills, industry experience and knowledge and length of service.      能 、行業經驗和知識及服務年期 。考慮該等
In considering these perspectives on diversity, the Company will also take         多元化層面時 ，本公司亦不時基於其業務模
into account factors based on its business model and specific needs from           型及具體需求考慮各項因素 。
time to time.


The Nomination Committee will be responsible for identifying suitably qualified    提名委員會將負責物色具備合適資格的候選
candidates to become members of the Board and, in carrying out this                人擔任董事會成員 ，並在履行此職責時充分
responsibility, will give adequate consideration to the Board Diversity Policy.    考慮董事會成員多元化政策 。截至二零二零
During the year ended 31 December 2020, the Nomination Committee and               年十二月三十一日止年度 ，提名委員會及薪
the Remuneration Committee had taken the Board Diversity Policy into               酬委員會已就委任董事事宜充分考慮董事會
account in relation to the appointment of directors of the Company.                成員多元化政策 。

appropriate skills, knowledges and experience to promote and achieve better        同之適當技能 、知識及經驗 ，可促進及提升

by the same individual. The division of responsibilities between the Chairman   一人同時兼任 。主席與行政總裁之間職責之
and CEO should be clearly established and set out in writing.                   分工應清楚界定並以書面列載 。


The current Chairman and CEO of the Company is Mr. Li Jialin. The Board         本公司現時之主席兼行政總裁為李佳林先
believes that vesting the roles of both Chairman and CEO in the same            生 。董事會相信 ，將主席及行政總裁之角色
person will not impair the balance of power and authority between the           歸屬同一人不會損害本公司董事與管理層之
Directors and the management of the Company and considers that this             間的權力及權限平衡 ，並認為此架構將可讓
structure will enable the Group to make and implement decisions promptly        本集團能及時及有效地制訂及推行決策 。董
and effectively. The Directors will meet regularly to consider major matters    事將定期舉行會議以考慮影響本公司營運之

Appointment and Re-election of Directors                                          委任及重選董事

to each of them will be reviewed by the Board each year.                          執行董事之薪酬 。

directors are laid down in the Company’s Articles of Association. Pursuant        司之組織章程細則中均有規定 。根據本公司

Risk Management and Internal Control                                             風險管理及內部監控
The Board is responsible for maintaining appropriate and effective risk          董事會負責維護適當有效之風險管理及內部
management and internal control systems.to safeguard shareholders’               監控制度 ，以保障股東的投資 ，更有效控制
investments and have a better control on the business and operation of           本公司之業務及營運 。董事會亦負責每年檢
the Company. The Board is also responsible for reviewing the effectiveness       討風險管理及內部監控制度之成效 。
of its risk management and internal control systems on an annual basis.


The Company has maintained a tailored governance structure with defined          本公司設有切合本身所需之管治架構 ，明確
lines of responsibility and appropriate delegation of responsibility and         劃分職責 ，並向高級管理層轉授適當責任及
authority to the senior management. As a leading and comprehensive ICT           授權 。作為領先而全面的資訊通訊科技產品
products distributor, our Group has many well-known suppliers and vendors        分銷商 ，本集團有許多知名供應商及承辦商
as well as extensive downstream channel partners. In order to cooperate          以及大量下游渠道夥伴。為與各方充分合
with different parties, our senior management team will be delegated to          作 ，我們的高級管理層團隊將獲轉授不同責
take different responsibility such as product management, supply chain           任 ，各自負責產品管理 、供應鏈管理 、營運
management, operational management and financial management, etc., to            管理及財務管理等範疇 ，確保本集團業務運
ensure that the Group has a smooth business operation to reduce risk and         作暢順 ，減低可能出現之風險及不確定因素 。
uncertainties that may be caused.


The management of the Company is responsible for designing, implementing         本公司之管理層負責設計 、執行及監察本集
and monitoring the Group’s risk management and internal control framework,       團之風險管理及內部監控架構 ，範圍涵蓋所
covering all material controls including financial, operational and compliance   有重大監控 ，包括財務 、營運及合規監控 。
controls. The risk management and internal control framework also provides       風險管理及內部監控架構亦可識別及管理風
for identification and management of risk.                                       險。


The management also conducts periodic meetings and independent reviews           管理層亦針對個別部門之運作舉行定期會議
on the operations of individual divisions to identify any irregularities and     並進行獨立檢討 ，以識別有否任何違規事項
risks, develops action plans and recommendations to address the identified       及風險 、制定行動計劃 、提供推薦建議處理
risks, and reports to the Audit Committee on any key findings. The Audit         所識別之風險 ，並向審核委員會匯報重大發
Committee, in turn, reports to the Board on any material issues and makes        現 。審核委員會則向董事會匯報任何重大事
recommendations to the Board.                                                    項並提供推薦建議 。


The Board acknowledged that it is their responsibilities to maintain an          董事會確認其有責任維護有效之風險管理及
effective risk management and internal control systems. Such systems             內部監控制度 。該等制度旨在管理而非消除
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve      未能實現業務目標之風險 ，且僅可就防止重
business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute            大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對之保證 。
assurance against material misstatement or loss.

Share option scheme of the Company                                                      本公司之購股權計劃
The share option scheme of the Company adopted on 17 April 2002 (the                    於二零零二年四月十七日採納之本公司購股
“Old Scheme”) has been expired. The Company adopted a new share option                  權 計 劃（「 舊 計 劃 」）已 屆 滿 。 本 公 司 於 二 零
scheme (the “New Scheme”) on 23 June 2015 for the purpose of providing                  一五年六月二十三日採納一項新購股權計劃
incentives and rewards to eligible participants for their contribution to the           （「 新 計 劃 」）， 旨 在 就 合 資 格 參 與 者 為 本 集 團
Group. During the year under review, no share options have been granted                 作出之貢獻提供激勵和嘉獎 。於回顧年度概

1. General Information                                                         1. 一般資料
     VSTECS Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (together     偉 仕 佳 杰 控 股 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」）及 其
     the “Group”) are principally engaged in the development of information     附 屬 公 司（ 統 稱「 本 集 團 」）之 主 要 業 務 為
     technology (“IT”) product channel and provision of technical solution      開發資訊科技產品渠道及提供技術解決
     integration services. The three major business segments are enterprise     方案整和服務 。三項主要業務為企業系
     systems which provides enterprise system tools for IT infrastructure       統（ 提 供 用 於 資 訊 科 技 基 礎 設 施 之 企 業
     and IT infrastructure design and implementation, training, maintenance     系統工具及資訊科技基礎設施設計及執
     and support services; Consumer electronics which provides finished         行 、 培 訓 、 維 修 及 支 援 服 務 ）； 消 費 電
     IT products; and cloud computing which provides cloud computing            子（ 提 供 資 訊 科 技 產 品 整 機 ）； 及 雲 計 算
     solutions and services.                                                    （提供雲計算解決方案和服務）。


     The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in the Cayman      本公司為在開曼群島註冊成立之有限公
     Islands. Its principal place of business is at Unit 3312, 33rd Floor,      司 。其主要營業地點位於香港干諾道中
     China Merchants Tower, Shun Tak Centre, 200 Connaught Road                 200 號 信 德 中 心 招 商 局 大 廈 33 樓 3312
     Central, Hong Kong.                                                        室。

   2.6 Goodwill                                                                   2.6 商譽
        Goodwill represents the excess of:                                              商譽指以下兩者之間的差額：


        (i)      the aggregate of the fair value of the consideration                   (i)    所轉讓代價的公平值、被
                 transferred, the amount of any non-controlling interest in                    收購方任何非控股權益的金
                 the acquiree and the fair value of the Group’s previously                     額及本集團此前於被收購方
                 held equity interest in the acquiree; over                                    所持股本權益的公平值之總
                                                                                               和；超過


        (ii)     the net fair value of the acquiree’s identifiable assets and           (ii)   被收購方於收購日期所計量
                 liabilities measured as at the acquisition date.                              可識別資產及負債的淨公
                                                                                               平值 。


        When (ii) is greater than (i), then this excess is recognised                   當 (ii) 大 於 (i) 時 ， 此 差 額 立 即 於 損
        immediately in profit or loss as a gain on a bargain purchase.                  益賬中確認為議價收購收益 。


        Goodwill is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses.                  商譽按成本扣除累計減值虧損列
        Goodwill arising on a business combination is allocated to each                 賬 。業務合併所產生之商譽分配
        cash generating unit, or groups of cash generating units, that is               至預期可從合併的協同效應中受
        expected to benefit from the synergies of the combination and                   惠之各個或各組現金產生單位，
        is tested annually for impairment (see note 2.7).                               並每年作減值測試（見附註2.7）。


        On disposal of a cash generating unit during the year, any                      於年內出售現金產生單位時，已
        attributable amount of purchased goodwill is included in the                    購入商譽應佔之任何金額乃於計
        calculation of the gain or loss on disposal.                                    算出售盈虧時納入其中 。

            Subsequent to initial recognition, the amount initially recognised            交易下提供類似服務收取之費用

      2.18 Provisions and contingent liabilities                                    2.18 撥備及或然負債
            Provisions are recognised for liabilities of uncertain timing                 當本集團或本公司因過往事件而
            or amount when the Group or the Company has a legal or                        出現法律或推定責任，且履行有
            constructive obligation arising as a result of a past event, it is            關責任可能需要經濟利益流出，
            probable that an outflow of economic benefits will be required                並可對此作出可靠的估計時，即
            to settle the obligation and a reliable estimate can be made.                 會對時間或金額不確定之負債確
            Where the time value of money is material, provisions are stated              認撥備。倘貨幣時間價值屬重
            at the present value of the expenditure expected to settle the                大，則撥備按預期履行責任所需
            obligation.                                                                   開支之現值列賬 。

            Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic benefits                 倘不大可能需要經濟利益流出，
            will be required, or the amount cannot be estimated reliably,                 或無法可靠估計有關金額，則該
            the obligation is disclosed as a contingent liability, unless the             責任乃披露為或然負債，除非經
            probability of outflow of economic benefits is remote. Possible               濟利益流出之可能性極微 。可能
            obligations, whose existence will only be confirmed by the                    承 擔 之 責 任（ 其 存 在 與 否 將 僅 由 是
            occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future events are                 否發生一項或多項未來事件確認）
            also disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the probability of            亦披露為或然負債，除非經濟利
            outflow of economic benefits is remote.                                       益流出之可能性極微 。

        At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether the                       本集團於合約初始評估有關合約
        contract is, or contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a                 是否屬於一項租賃或包含一項租
        lease if the contract conveys the right to control the use of an                 賃 。倘合約授予權利以代價為交
        identified asset for a period of time in exchange for consideration.             換在某一時期內控制使用已識別
        Control is conveyed where the customer has both the right to                     資產，則該合約屬於租賃或包含
        direct the use of the identified asset and to obtain substantially               租賃 。倘客戶同時有權指示已識
        all of the economic benefits from that use.                                      別資產之用途及從該用途獲得絕
                                                                                         大部分經濟利益，則控制權予以
                                                                                         轉移 。


        (i)      As a lessee                                                             (i)   作為承租人
                 Where the contract contains lease component(s) and non-                       倘合約載有租賃部分及非租

                 non-lease components and accounts for each lease                              獨劃分非租賃部分 ，並將各
                 component and any associated non-lease components                             租賃部分及任何相關非租賃
                 as a single lease component for all leases.                                   部分入賬 ，列為所有租賃的

                 At the lease commencement date, the Group recognises                          於租賃開始日期 ，本集團確
                 a right-of-use asset and a lease liability, except for short-                 認使用權資產及租賃負債（惟
                 term leases that have a lease term of 12 months or less                       租賃期限為12個月或以下之
                 and leases of low-value assets. When the Group enters                         短期租賃及低價值資產租賃
                 into a lease in respect of a low-value asset, the Group                       除 外 ）。 當 本 集 團 就 低 價 值
                 decides whether to capitalise the lease on a lease-by-lease                   資產訂立租賃時 ，本集團會
                 basis. The lease payments associated with those leases                        按租賃基準決定是否將租賃
                 which are not capitalised are recognised as an expense                        撥充資本 。與該等租賃有關

                                                                                               開支 。

      3.2 Capital risk management                                                   3.2 資本風險管理
            The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard                 本集團管理資本之目的為確保本
            the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order                   集團有能力持續營運，以為股東
            to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other                    提供回報及為其他持份者提供利
            stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to                  益，以及維持最佳資本架構以減
            reduce the cost of capital.                                                   低資本成本 。

            In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group               為維持或調整資本架構，本集
            may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, return               團或會調整派付予股東之股息金
            capital to shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to                   額、向股東發還資本、發行新股
            reduce debt.                                                                  份或售資減債 。

                    The fair value of forward contracts in Level 2 is determined                       第二層級遠期合約之公平值

                    from the relevant government yield curve as at the end                             用之貼現率按報告期末之有

                    spread.                                                                            夠固定信貸息差得出 。

                    amount that the Group would realise, taking into                                   集團可變現之估計金額 ， 並
                    account the interest rates and credit worthiness of the                            經計及對手方之投資費率及
                    counterparties.                                                                    信用 。

4. Accounting Estimates and Judgements                                       4. 會計估計及判斷
   (a) Estimated write-down of inventories to net realisable                   (a) 估 計 存 貨 撇 減 至 可 變 現 淨
       value                                                                       值
       The Group writes down inventories to net realisable value based               本集團根據存貨可變現性之評估
       on an assessment of the realisability of inventories. Write-down              撇減存貨至可變現淨值 。倘出現
       of inventories is recorded to the extent where events or changes              顯示有關結餘可能未能變現之事
       in circumstances indicate that the balances may not be realised.              件或情況變動，則會以此為限將
                                                                                     存貨撇減記賬 。


       The identification of write-down requires the use of judgement                識別撇減須運用判斷及估計 。倘
       and estimates. Where the expectation is different from the                    預期有別於原有估計，則有關差
       original estimate, such difference will impact the carrying value             額將影響該估計出現變動期間之
       of inventories and write-down of inventories in the period in                 存貨賬面值及存貨撇減 。
       which such estimate are changed.


   (b) Estimated loss allowance of trade receivables                           (b) 估計貿易應收款項虧損撥備
       The Group measures loss allowance of trade receivables at an                  本集團按照金額相等於貿易應收
       amount equal to lifetime expected credit loss of trade receivables.           款項整個存續期預期信貸虧損計
                                                                                     算貿易應收款項的虧損撥備 。


       The measurement of loss allowance of trade receivables requires               計量貿易應收款項的虧損撥備須
       the use of judgement and estimates. Where the expectation is                  運用判斷及估計 。倘預期有別於
       different from the original estimate, such difference will impact             原有估計，則有關差額將影響該
       the carrying value of trade receivables and the expected credit               估計出現變動期間之貿易應收款
       loss in the period in which such estimates are changed.                       項賬面值及預期信貸虧損 。


   (c) Estimated impairment of goodwill                                        (c) 估計商譽減值
       The Group tests annually whether goodwill has suffered any                    本 集 團 每 年 根 據 附 註 2.7 所 述 會 計

       in note 2.7. The recoverable amounts of the relevant cash                     關現金產生單位之可收回金額已
       generating units have been determined based on value-in-use                   根據使用價值計算法釐定 。該等
       calculations. These calculations require the use of estimates                 計算法需要運用估計（見附註6）。
       (see note 6).


       In assessing the value-in-use of the goodwill, management                     評估商譽之使用價值時，管理

       factors. Estimates of future cash flows are highly subjective                 設。估計未來現金流量十分主

      reference to sales generated by those segments and the expenses            額及該等分部產生之開支分配至該等分
      incurred by those segments.                                                部。

      including items specifically attributed to individual segments, such as    績 ，包括個別分部特別應佔之項目 ，例

      within the segment.                                                        行政費用 。
